2026-10-11T18:02:38.9067999Z  	DECLARE
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9068147Z  	 c jsonb; r jsonb; ledger_state text; i record; t record;
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9068356Z  	 wanted_task uuid; wanted_node uuid; wanted_target uuid; wanted_epoch bigint;

2026-10-11T18:02:38.9069413Z  	 SELECT wi.*, wv.version_id AS original_version_id, wd.name AS original_name INTO STRICT i
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9069737Z  	 FROM "journal_migration_cfcc4de1ae5844c5ba52f1cd3ee7f1fa".workflow_instances wi
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9070125Z  	 JOIN "journal_migration_cfcc4de1ae5844c5ba52f1cd3ee7f1fa".workflow_definitions wd ON wd.app_id=wi.app_id AND wd.id=wi.flow_id
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9070620Z  	 JOIN "journal_migration_cfcc4de1ae5844c5ba52f1cd3ee7f1fa".workflow_versions wv ON wv.app_id=wi.app_id AND wv.flow_id=wi.flow_id AND wv.version=wi.definition_version
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9070766Z  	 WHERE wi.app_id=NEW.app_id AND wi.id=NEW.instance_id;
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9070906Z  	 IF (c->>'ProtocolVersion')::integer IS DISTINCT FROM 2
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9071053Z  	 OR (c->>'CommandID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.command_id
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9071183Z  	 OR (c->>'AppID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.app_id
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9071334Z  	 OR (c->>'InstanceID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.instance_id
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9071459Z  	 OR (c->>'ActorID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.actor_id
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9071578Z  	 OR c->>'Action' IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.action
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9071757Z  	 OR (c->>'SchemaVersion')::bigint IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.schema_version
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9071925Z  	 OR (c->>'RecordVersion')::bigint IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.record_version
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9072050Z  	 OR (c->>'TableID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM i.table_id
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9072171Z  	 OR (c->>'ViewID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM i.view_id
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9072300Z  	 OR (c->>'RecordID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM i.record_id
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9072415Z  	 OR (c->>'FlowID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM i.flow_id
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9072571Z  	 OR (c->>'VersionID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM i.original_version_id
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9072771Z  	 OR (c->>'DefinitionVersion')::bigint IS DISTINCT FROM i.definition_version
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9073136Z  	 OR NEW.sequence IS DISTINCT FROM (c->>'ExpectedSequence')::bigint + (CASE WHEN NEW.outcome='success' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) THEN
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9073349Z  	  RAISE EXCEPTION 'workflow journal identity mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23514';
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9073431Z  	 END IF;
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9073636Z  	 -- INSERT is inside the original projection callback, before terminal ledger
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9073839Z  	 -- update. Backfill, however, must only accept already confirmed old events.
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9073969Z  	 IF TG_OP='UPDATE' OR ledger_state<>'pending' THEN
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9074116Z  	  IF ledger_state IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.outcome OR r IS NULL
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9074268Z  	  OR (r->>'CommandID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.command_id
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9074389Z  	  OR r->>'Outcome' IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.outcome
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9074526Z  	  OR (r->>'Sequence')::bigint IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.sequence
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9074671Z  	  OR (r->>'ProofID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.proof_id THEN
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9074915Z  	   RAISE EXCEPTION 'workflow journal terminal receipt mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23514';
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9074996Z  	  END IF;
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9075079Z  	 END IF;
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9075189Z  	 wanted_task:=nullif(c->>'TaskID','')::uuid;
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9075299Z  	 wanted_epoch:=(c->>'TaskEpoch')::bigint;
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9075430Z  	 wanted_target:=nullif(c->>'TargetNodeID','')::uuid;
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9075646Z  	 IF wanted_task IS NOT NULL THEN

2026-10-11T18:02:38.9076065Z  	  IF FOUND THEN
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9076482Z  	   IF (t.app_id,t.instance_id,t.assignee_id,t.activation_epoch) IS DISTINCT FROM (NEW.app_id,NEW.instance_id,NEW.actor_id,wanted_epoch) THEN
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9076714Z  	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'workflow journal original task mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23514';
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9076905Z  	   END IF;
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9077001Z  	   wanted_node:=t.node_id;
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9077103Z  	  ELSIF NEW.outcome<>'no_effect' THEN
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9077321Z  	   RAISE EXCEPTION 'workflow journal original task missing' USING ERRCODE='23514';
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9077505Z  	  END IF;
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9077583Z  	 END IF;
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9077811Z  	 wanted_source:=CASE WHEN TG_OP='UPDATE' THEN 'legacy_last_known' ELSE 'captured' END;
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9078017Z  	 -- Explicit conflicting caller values are rejected, not silently repaired.
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9078210Z  	 IF (NEW.table_id IS NOT NULL AND NEW.table_id IS DISTINCT FROM i.table_id)
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9078395Z  	 OR (NEW.view_id IS NOT NULL AND NEW.view_id IS DISTINCT FROM i.view_id)
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9078597Z  	 OR (NEW.record_id IS NOT NULL AND NEW.record_id IS DISTINCT FROM i.record_id)
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9078767Z  	 OR (NEW.flow_id IS NOT NULL AND NEW.flow_id IS DISTINCT FROM i.flow_id)
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9079009Z  	 OR (NEW.version_id IS NOT NULL AND NEW.version_id IS DISTINCT FROM i.original_version_id)
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9079311Z  	 OR (NEW.definition_version IS NOT NULL AND NEW.definition_version IS DISTINCT FROM i.definition_version)
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9079501Z  	 OR (NEW.task_id IS NOT NULL AND NEW.task_id IS DISTINCT FROM wanted_task)
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9079712Z  	 OR (NEW.task_epoch IS NOT NULL AND NEW.task_epoch IS DISTINCT FROM wanted_epoch)
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9079888Z  	 OR (NEW.node_id IS NOT NULL AND NEW.node_id IS DISTINCT FROM wanted_node)
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9080121Z  	 OR (NEW.target_node_id IS NOT NULL AND NEW.target_node_id IS DISTINCT FROM wanted_target)
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9080326Z  	 OR (NEW.flow_name IS NOT NULL AND NEW.flow_name IS DISTINCT FROM i.original_name)
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9080593Z  	 OR (NEW.flow_name_source IS NOT NULL AND NEW.flow_name_source IS DISTINCT FROM wanted_source) THEN
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9080833Z  	  RAISE EXCEPTION 'workflow journal supplied metadata mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23514';
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9080915Z  	 END IF;

2026-10-11T18:02:38.9081284Z  	 NEW.flow_id:=i.flow_id; NEW.version_id:=i.original_version_id;
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9081482Z  	 NEW.definition_version:=i.definition_version; NEW.task_id:=wanted_task;

2026-10-11T18:02:38.9081910Z  	 NEW.flow_name:=i.original_name; NEW.flow_name_source:=wanted_source;
2026-10-11T18:02:38.9081992Z  	 RETURN NEW;
